The [caffeine and mobility audit](../2025-2029/Food-Experiment-Round-1-Caffeine-and-Mobility-Nov24.md) links 28 originals from this period. The [manualized treatment trial](Source-Text/2015-PMID26501499-Manualized-Caffeine-Reduction-and-Fatigue-Scope.txt), [manual-only withdrawal composite](Source-Text/2018-PMID30580203-Caffeine-Taper-and-Withdrawal-Composite.txt) and [Parkinson fatigue definition](Source-Text/2016-PMID26879133-Parkinson-Fatigue-Case-Definition-and-Apathy.txt) distinguish clinical and grouped fatigue-related outcomes.

The [cognition and nutrition audit](../2025-2029/Food-Experiment-Round-1-Cognition-and-Nutrition-Nov24.md) links 29 dated originals in this period, including the [stilbene review](Source-Text/2015-PMID26180583-Stilbene-Review-Grape-and-Olive-Source-Lineage.txt), [isolated sesamol comparison](Source-Text/2015-PMID25829772-Sesamol-Cognition-and-Sesame-Oil-Scope.txt) and [cisplatin hypoalgesia comparison](Source-Text/2015-PMID26113793-Curcumin-Cisplatin-Thermal-Hypoalgesia.txt). The separately traced [2018 case](Source-Text/2018-IJAR7678-Panchakarma-Parkinson-Case.txt) documents residual tremor during a bundled regimen.

The [caffeine and L-theanine fatigue comparison](Source-Text/2015-PMID25761837-Dodd-Theanine-Caffeine-and-Mental-Fatigue.txt) preserves the inconclusive treatment term and descriptive means. [Period observations](Observations/2015-Caffeine-Theanine-and-Fatigue-Contrasts.md).

The [brewed green tea conversation](Cultural-and-Experiential/Conversations/2019-Reddit-Brewed-Green-Tea-and-Medication-Wearing-Off.txt) preserves a first-person Parkinson symptom or medication-timing observation with its preparation and limitations.

The [Round 1 priority-five source audit](../2025-2029/Food-Experiment-Round-1-Priority-Five-Body-Replay-Nov22.md) links whole-source checks for synthetic curcumin, ginger, nigella seed and the other publications examined in this period.

The [2015 Samad rice bran oil original](Source-Text/2015-PMID28911693-Samad-Rice-Bran-Oil-Motor-Outcome-Scope.txt) identifies a 16-animal experiment and distinguishes its chewing and coordination outcomes from clinical rigidity and dystonia. The [2017 companion note](Source-Text/2017-PMID28374238-Samad-Haleem-Rice-Bran-Oil-Access.txt) records the abstract and original-body gap.

The [fibromyalgia saffron extract trial](Source-Text/2018-PMID30456199-Shakiba-Saffron-Duloxetine-Fibromyalgia.txt), its [prospective registration](Source-Text/Trials/IRCT201604261556N91.txt), and the [burning-mouth crocin trial](Source-Text/2019-PMID31763210-Pakfetrat-Crocin-Citalopram-Burning-Mouth.txt) record their active-drug comparisons and outcome gaps.

The [dried-date and apathy source review](../2025-2029/Food-Experiment-Dried-Date-Apathy-Originals-Nov22.md) links original article summaries, exact access routes, and the evidence still needed for the Parkinson claim.

The [raw-fig and resting tremor review](../2025-2029/Food-Experiment-Fig-Rest-Tremor-Originals-Nov22.md) links original source summaries and access notes filed in this period.

The [Round 1 indexed original audit](../2025-2029/Food-Experiment-Indexed-Primary-Originals-Nov22.md) links the authenticated original bodies and the remaining [Parkinson gait imagery](Source-Text/2015-PMID25765327-Lau-Parkinson-Gait-Original-Access.txt) and [Lewy body sleep case](Source-Text/2019-PMID30852516-Londos-Lewy-Body-Sleep-Original-Access.txt) source gaps.

The [IAGG original extract](Source-Text/2017-PMC6183407-IAGG-Gerontology-Original-Access.txt) verifies printed pp. 1293–1294. The [FEBS poster correction](Source-Text/2019-PMC6886306-FEBS-Poster-Corrigendum.txt) is available alongside the [poster collection access record](Source-Text/2019-PMC6610278-FEBS-Original-Access.txt).

The [2014 AES meeting supplement](Source-Text/PMC4591846.txt) was published in 2015. Its access note preserves the member and complete-volume gaps.

[2016 FEBS poster exposure review](Source-Text/2016-PMC7164006-FEBS-Poster-Exposure-Scope.txt), [Talbina customary-use account](Cultural-and-Experiential/Traditional-Practices/2016-FEBS-Talbina-Illness-and-Bereavement.txt), and [2016 neurology meeting access record](Source-Text/PMC7159757.txt).

New source records on oral outcomes and symptom measurement include [Russo SCA3 Swallowing Scales](Source-Text/2015-PMID30363545-Russo-SCA3-Swallowing-Scales.txt).

The [Café-PD capsule trial](Source-Text/2017-PMID28954882-Postuma-Cafe-PD-Adverse-Outcomes.txt) separates measured dyskinesia, sleep outcomes and aggregate adverse events. The [Phillips diet pilot](Source-Text/2018-PMID30098269-Phillips-Diet-Adverse-Outcomes.txt) reports combined tremor or rigidity worsening alongside its nonmotor scores. The assigned exposures were caffeine capsules and whole diets, respectively.

The [posture and saliva observation](Observations/Posture-Wearables-and-Saliva-Outcomes.md) cross-links the [Iijima case lineage](Source-Text/2015-PMID29468202-Iijima-Antecollis-Case-Lineage.txt), [Yoshii posture cohort](Source-Text/2016-PMID26835153-Yoshii-Posture-Angles-and-Drooling-Context.txt), [wearable viewpoint](Source-Text/2016-PMID27003779-Van-Uem-Wearable-Measurement-and-Drooling-Context.txt), [skin review](Source-Text/2016-PMID30363435-Skorvanek-Skin-and-Drooling-Context.txt), and [Swedish orofacial registry](Source-Text/2017-PMID28798882-Fereshtehnejad-Registry-Drooling.txt).

The [oral outcomes and Mucuna observations](Observations/Oral-Outcome-Distinctions-and-Mucuna-Products.md) link the [Salivagram study](Source-Text/2017-PMID29354567-Park-Salivagram-and-Swallowing.txt), the [review of swallowing measures](Source-Text/2017-PMID28375450-Patel-Dysphagia-Instrument-Review.txt) and the [Mucuna product assay](Source-Text/2017-PMID28922612-Soumyanath-Mucuna-Label-Comparison.txt).

The [drooling and saliva measurements](Observations/Drooling-Swallowing-and-Saliva-Sampling.md) compare the [Sohag cohort](Source-Text/2018-PMID30416335-Mohamed-Subtype-Drooling-and-Swallowing.txt), [Zhejiang cohort](Source-Text/2016-Xie-Videofluoroscopy-and-Drooling.txt), [Hamburg cohort](Source-Text/2017-PMID29178420-Nienstedt-Aspiration-and-Drooling.txt) and [S4 feasibility report](Source-Text/2018-PMID30248065-Chahine-S4-Saliva-Collection.txt). Their outcomes include oral saliva loss, swallowing and collected sample volume.

The [Barbe diary registration](Source-Text/Trials/DRKS00012458.txt) and [publication](Source-Text/2018-PMID30028552-Barbe-Symptom-Diary-and-Meals.txt) distinguish hourly dry mouth, drooling, swallowing and food activity. [Period observations](Observations/Saliva-Diary-Motor-Subtypes-and-Patient-Accounts.md) link the adjacent outcome measures and patient reports.

The [P167 food-allergy abstract](Source-Text/2015-PMC4412414-P167-Fig-Allergy-Case.txt) separates unspecified ingested fig from fresh and dried skin-test materials. The published case gives no separate drooling report.

The [divided-attention registry](Source-Text/Trials/ISRCTN11045864.txt), [2017 congress abstract](Source-Text/2017-MDS-111-Reynolds-Divided-Attention-and-Drooling.txt) and [2018 article](Source-Text/2018-PMID29785679-Reynolds-Divided-Attention-and-Drooling.txt) report one Parkinson study. Its drooling frequency and severity outcomes differ. The [2019 wrist-cue pilot](Source-Text/2019-PMID31662884-McNaney-Wrist-Cueing-and-Drooling.txt) contrasts the delayed-start and pooled before–after estimates.

The [2018 DUMP umbrella registration](Source-Text/Trials/EUPAS26319.txt) documents three French prescribing studies. The separately registered cross-sectional component links to the [2025 Parkinson cohort](../2025-2029/Source-Text/2025-PMID40062736-DUMP-Ginger-Use-and-Registration.txt).

The [2017 ginger-extract report](Source-Text/2017-Dass-Ginger-Extract-Rhodes-and-Heartburn.txt) gives a before-and-after pregnancy nausea score and four heartburn reports. The later pharmacology abstract appears to describe the same cohort.

The [2018 ginger contractility study](Source-Text/2018-PMC5954582-Ginger-Small-Intestinal-Contractility.txt) distinguished prior oral exposure from direct application to excised rat small intestine. The [period’s cultural sources](Cultural-and-Experiential/README.md) include the Trinidad plant survey and the Chinese zongzi wrapper survey.

The [2019 UEG poster collection](Source-Text/2019-PMC8454868-UEG-Poster-Food-Boundaries.txt) distinguishes pediatric herbal search mentions from measured dietary and curcumin comparisons. The [reproductive sciences abstract book](Source-Text/2016-PMC7104458-Reproductive-Abstract-Figure-Access-Extract.txt) retains an online figure-access gap. [NHANES abstract 2501](Source-Text/2018-PMC6798479-NHANES-Depression-Abstract-Identity.txt) has a separate record within a bundled PDF.

Food-related reference screens cover [TRPV1 and emesis](Source-Text/2015-PMC4843889-TRPV1-Emesis-Ginger-Scope.txt), [liver disease mixtures](Source-Text/2015-PMC4691073-Liver-Review-Ginger-and-Chinese-Sage-Scope.txt), [herbal compatibility](Source-Text/2016-PMC4846759-Danshen-Review-Ginger-Formula-Scope.txt), [sleep in kidney disease](Source-Text/2019-PMC6535156-CKD-Sleep-Review-Food-Scope.txt) and [minor digestive ailments](Source-Text/2019-PMC8127090-Minor-GI-Ailments-Exposure-Scope.txt).

The [2015 Daikenchuto report](Source-Text/2015-PMID27069528-Daikenchuto-Bloating-and-Pain.txt), [2018 chart review](Source-Text/2018-PMID29693001-Daikenchuto-Stool-Frequency-Chart-Review.txt) and [2019 poststroke reanalysis](Source-Text/2019-PMID31823650-Daikenchuto-Poststroke-Reanalysis.txt) address different outcomes and designs. The [2016 placebo trial registration](Source-Text/Trials/UMIN000020570.txt) gives the planned dose and endpoints for a separate study. A [2019 Cochrane review](Source-Text/2019-PMID30706461-Poststroke-Urinary-Moxibustion-Scope.txt) documents a topical ginger–salt and acupuncture method for urinary incontinence after stroke.

The [Mahapanchagavya treated series](Source-Text/2018-Usha-Singh-Mahapanchagavya-Epilepsy-Series.txt) reports compound seizure outcomes. The [earlier thesis entry](../2000-2004/Source-Text/2001-Usha-Apasmara-Thesis-Identity.txt) and later textual commentary point to likely reuse of the treatment group.

The [five selected Parkinson cases with delusional infestation](Source-Text/PMC6353320.txt) distinguish visual and tactile modality from the duration of the infestation syndrome.

The [confirmatory DLB donepezil trial](Source-Text/PMC4338565.txt) found an inconclusive placebo-adjusted hallucination and fluctuation composite. The separately recruited [postmarketing trial registration](Source-Text/Trials/NCT02345213.txt) prioritized global clinical change.

The [2015 FDA clinical review and label](Source-Text/2015-FDA-NDA203312-Levodopa-Phase-and-Hallucination-Events.txt) distinguish medication conversion adverse events and randomized maintenance in the ADVANCE-PD and ASCEND-PD programs.

The [2015 visual event and botanical observations](Observations/2015-Visual-Event-Definitions-and-Botanical-Preparations.md) compare episode reporting, drug conversion, diagnostic studies and the separately documented extract and ceremonial exposures.

The [2016 visual outcome and nilotinib observations](Observations/2016-Visual-Outcome-Definitions-and-Nilotinib-Lineage.md) distinguish clinical measures and trial families. The [published caregiver reports](Cultural-and-Experiential/Personal-Reports/2016-LBDA-Caregiver-Reports-Quoted-in-Clinical-Viewpoint.txt) document safety and daily care priorities. The [original source inventory](../../experiments/1000_see_what_sticks_food/followup/fig-priority-six-2016-nov19-96/source-receipts.tsv) lists the dated records.

The [2017 original source group](Observations/2017-Research-Visual-Endpoints-and-Fig-Constituents.md) compares cohort lineage, visual outcomes and dried fig mineral measurements. The [original source inventory](../../experiments/1000_see_what_sticks_food/followup/fig-priority-six-2017-nov19-95/source-receipts.tsv) links the dated source records.

The [Johns Hopkins psychosis cohort](Source-Text/PMC5945218.txt) and [EANM imaging abstract](Source-Text/2017-PMC7103094-Conference-Scope.txt) use different definitions of symptom change. [Endpoint and cohort observations](Observations/Psychosis-Remission-and-Visual-Symptom-Status.md).

The [2017 basal forebrain stimulation crossover](Source-Text/PMC5838617.txt) reports individual visual hallucination observations and an exploratory neuropsychiatric outcome. Its [2022 follow-up](../2020-2024/Source-Text/PMC9346244.txt) reuses the same cohort.

The [TOLEDO original report](Source-Text/2018-PMID30055903-TOLEDO-Visual-Adverse-Events.txt) records visual adverse events in a motor-fluctuation trial. [The separate refractory visual hallucination registration](Source-Text/Trials/NCT02702076.txt) identifies planned severity measures. [Registry and outcome observations](../2020-2024/Observations/Dried-Fig-Magnesium-and-Visual-Symptom-Timing.md).

The [USDA dried fig composition record](Source-Text/2019-FDC174665-Dried-Fig-Pantothenic-Acid.txt) identifies measured pantothenic acid in dried, uncooked figs. The later [brain-tissue and visual endpoint observations](../2020-2024/Observations/Dried-Fig-Constituent-and-Visual-Episode-Measures.md) trace where the food and clinical evidence diverge.

The [2016 flaxseed–fig caplet pilot](Source-Text/2016-Tofighi-Flaxseed-Fig-Caplet-Pilot.txt) records a single-cohort comparison against baseline. The original visual records distinguish [SNCA phenotype](Source-Text/2015-PMID26350119-SNCA-Duplication-and-Clinical-Phenotypes.txt), [cortical stimulation and imaging](Source-Text/2015-PMID26541688-Lewy-Body-Cortical-Excitability-and-Visual-Response.txt), [eye and perception measures](Source-Text/2015-PMID26599301-Parkinson-Oculo-Visual-Dysfunction-Review.txt), and [a visual imaging review](Source-Text/2016-PMID26744445-Neuroimaging-Differential-for-Visual-Hallucinations.txt) with [associated correspondence](Source-Text/2016-PMID27585699-Molecular-Imaging-Correspondence-on-Visual-Hallucinations.txt). Further comparisons cover [pulvinar imaging](Source-Text/2016-PMID27790244-Pulvinar-DWI-and-Visual-Hallucination-History.txt), [drug-associated visual adverse events](Source-Text/2017-PMID28777755-Amantadine-Extension-Visual-Adverse-Events.txt), [pulvinar molecular measurements](Source-Text/2018-PMID29570843-Pulvinar-Molecular-Markers-in-Lewy-Body-Dementia.txt), [neuroimaging synthesis](Source-Text/2019-PMID31608325-Parkinson-Dementia-and-Hallucination-Neuroimaging.txt), and [visual learning and disambiguation](Source-Text/2019-PMID31886459-Lewy-Body-Hallucinations-and-Visual-Prior-Knowledge.txt).

The [2016 fig-paste trial](Source-Text/2016-PMID27440682-Fig-Paste-and-Functional-Constipation.txt) estimates effects on colonic transit and individual bowel outcomes. [Preparation and protocol lineage observations](Observations/Fig-Paste-and-Clinical-Outcome-Boundaries.md).

The [original visual symptom and study-methods review](Observations/Fig-and-Visual-Hallucination-Source-Contexts.md) links dated clinical archives for 44 source comparisons and a separate fig extract convulsion paper.

The [full IGMC member P105](Source-Text/2016-PMID27454254-IGMC-Date-Fruit-P105.txt) identifies computed cancer pathways for literature-selected date constituents.

[Nutmeg volatile oil and hippocampal measures](Source-Text/PMC5634759.txt) · [PMC6869566](Source-Text/PMC6869566.txt) · [PMC6582384](Source-Text/PMC6582384.txt) · [PMC6661809](Source-Text/PMC6661809.txt) · [PMC4790408](Source-Text/PMC4790408.txt) · [PMC6296165](Source-Text/PMC6296165.txt).

[simulated vocal tremor and listener ratings](Source-Text/PMC4545074.txt) · [Parkinson genetic and olfaction comparison](Source-Text/PMC5567486.txt) · [ACID registration](Source-Text/Trials/NCT03419039.txt).

The [registered mixed-sage comparison](Source-Text/Trials/NCT03695003.txt) includes acute and longer-term mood assessments in healthy adults and a probable link to the 2021 results.

The [irsenontrine Lewy body dementia registration](Source-Text/Trials/NCT03467152.txt) includes aggregated results posted in 2022 and links to its later biomarker sub-study. The [pooled safinamide pain analysis](Source-Text/PMC5302028.txt) uses pain-treatment use and bodily discomfort outcomes.

The [COPD registration](Source-Text/Trials/NCT03796455.txt) and [NUTRIMAL registration](Source-Text/Trials/NCT03429491.txt) link to later mobility results. The [Smith corn-oil comparator trial](Source-Text/2015-PMID25994567-Smith-Corn-Oil-Muscle-Comparison.txt) documents muscle outcomes and the available adverse-event categories. The [Amaranthus liver paper](Source-Text/2015-Yilmaz-Ozden-Amaranthus-Olive-Oil-Hepatic-Vehicle.txt) and [female rat diazinon comparison](Source-Text/2016-Salehi-Corn-Oil-Diazinon-Carrier.txt) document injected oils in different laboratory contexts.

The [2018 European obesity congress source digest](Source-Text/2018-PMC7019188-ECO-Food-Exposure-Members.txt) keeps separate members and protocols. Linked records cover [MONICA coffee exposure](Source-Text/2017-PMID29381147-MONICA-Coffee-and-Adiposity.txt), [dysbiosis and beverage context](Source-Text/2018-PMC6276176-Faecal-Dysbiosis-and-Coffee-Context.txt), [the potato-fiber registration](Source-Text/Trials/NCT02957318.txt) and [the safflower and fish-oil crossover registration](Source-Text/Trials/NCT02647333.txt).

The [cold-pressed peanut-oil composition study](Source-Text/2016-Kostadinovic-Cold-Pressed-Peanut-Oil-Composition.txt), [ENHANce sarcopenia registration](Source-Text/Trials/NCT03649698.txt) and [Sultanpur customary massage record](Cultural-and-Experiential/Traditional-Practices/2018-Sultanpur-Peanut-Oil-Massage-Use.txt) describe different preparation roles and endpoints.

Four fennel texts describe [reserpine and bar-test activity](Source-Text/2018-Nemati-Fennel-Oil-Reserpine-Motor-Tests.txt), [aerial juice toxicity](Source-Text/2018-Al-Hizab-Fennel-Aerial-Juice-Toxicity.txt), [roasted-fruit volatiles](Source-Text/2018-PMID30268004-Roasted-Fennel-Fruit-Volatiles.txt) and [whole crushed seed in mouse feed](Source-Text/2019-PMID31680077-Fennel-Seed-Diet-and-Mouse-Activity.txt). [Comparative observations](../2020-2024/Observations/Fennel-Preparation-and-Resting-Tremor.md).

The [2015 naringin experiment](Source-Text/2015-PMID26878791-Naringin-Prevention-and-Restoration.txt) tests prevention and recovery of tissue markers in mice. The [grapefruit and lavender aroma registration](Source-Text/Trials/UMIN000021236.txt) records planned immediate human outcomes. [Comparative observations](../2020-2024/Observations/Grapefruit-Preparation-and-Tremor.md).

The [2018 chicken crossover registration](Source-Text/Trials/UMIN000033042.txt), [2019 chicken and training registration](Source-Text/Trials/UMIN000038253.txt) and [2019 retort composition experiment](Source-Text/2019-Kouriki-Nagatomo-Chicken-Dipeptide-Retention-Extract.txt) document different prepared exposures. [Trial and preparation observations](Observations/Food-Experiment-Chicken-Fatigue-Nov19-24.md).

- [Lumbee cultural context and supplementary access gap](Cultural-and-Experiential/Local-Knowledge/2017-Lumbee-Calming-Tea-Context.txt), linked to the existing factual extract.

- [Broad-bean belief and practitioner opinion](Cultural-and-Experiential/Traditional-Practices/2015-Broad-Bean-Belief-and-Practitioner-Concern.txt).
- [Public sprouted-fava resting-tremor account](Cultural-and-Experiential/Personal-Reports/2019-Sprouted-Fava-Perceived-Rest-Tremor-Relief.txt).
- [Cultural-source comparison with the preceding period](Observations/Cultural-and-Experiential-Tremor.md).

Additional dated source profiles: [2016 reproductive figures and access hold](Source-Text/2016-PMC7104458-Reproductive-Abstract-Figure-Access-Extract.txt), [2016 animal-model egg specimens](Source-Text/2016-PMC7150119-Animal-Model-Egg-Specimen-Extract.txt), [2016 commercial walnut hydrosol](Source-Text/2016-PMID27335580-Moravej-Walnut-Hydrosol-Extract.txt), [2017 French botanical dictionary](Source-Text/2017-PMC7151859-French-Dictionary-Culinary-Botanicals-Extract.txt), [2018 Miller author abstract](Source-Text/2018-PMID29634989-Miller-Walnut-Oil-Extinction-Abstract-Extract.txt), [2019 glycemic companion report](Source-Text/2019-Aghasadeghi-Walnut-Oil-Glycemia-Extract.txt), [2019 egg-allergy abstracts](Source-Text/2019-PMC7095959-Pediatric-Egg-Allergy-Abstract-Extract.txt) and [2019 cinnamon motor study](Source-Text/2019-PMID31119595-Cinnamon-GDNF-Motor-Extract.txt). [Observations and study-family limits](Observations/Food-Experiment-Source-Context-Nov17-10.md).

January 1, 2015 through December 31, 2019.
